\cite[citet]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{Mairson:2004}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}} explains that function \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Xor}}}}}}} is problematic because it is non-linear, in the sense that it uses the bits and \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b1}}}}}}} more than once in the same expression.
To restore linearity, Mairson proposes to explicitly implement a fanout gate \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Copy}}}}}}} that takes a single bit and returns two equivalent bits:
\par{\@listings{\@@listings@block{11}{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{1\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line 1}}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.33}{fun}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Pair}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Copy}}}}}}} is used this way: $$\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Copy}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ the inner expression \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$e$}}}}}} by assigning \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b}}}}}}}'s type (Boolean value) to \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b1}}}}}}} and \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b2}}}}}}}.
Therefore, \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$e$}}}}}} can use two instances \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b1}}}}}}} and \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b2}}}}}}} of the same Boolean bit \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{b}}}}}}}.
Let us re-implement \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Xor}}}}}}} using \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{Copy}}}}}}}:

Again, we start by encoding a fluent API for our example language $(a^{4})^{*}$ and then generalize the construction for any FSM in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{algorithm:church}.
The first step of the encoding is identical to Step I of bit shuffling (described in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:shuffling:1}), so we can continue straight to the second step.
\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Step II: Encoding FSM States as Vectors of Church Booleans}
\par In Step I (\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:shuffling:1}) we constructed mapping $\beta:Q\rightarrow\{0,1\}^{2}$ from states to bit vectors.
Function $\beta$ is used to encode the FSM states $Q$ as Church Boolean vectors:

$$\boldsymbol{f}^{a}(b_{2}b_{1})=\langle b_{2}\oplus b_{1},\neg b_{1}\rangle$$Function $\boldsymbol{f}^{a}$ can be encoded in SML using Church Booleans and Mairson's Let $w=a^{n}$ and let $p_{n}$ be the last state in $w$'s run.

\par The terminal function \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}\@listingLiterate{\textdollar}}}}} should check that the last state in the run is accepting.
Recall that in our example, this condition is verified by $$g^{8}(b_{2}b_{1})=\neg b_{2}\wedge\neg b_{1}$$as $g^{8}(\beta(q))=1\Leftrightarrow q\in F$.

L((a^{4})^{*)}}}}{\@listingGroup{}{$$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{}}\textasteriskcentered)\@lst@endline}}{}\@@toccaption{{\lx@tag[ ]{{17}}{A Church-encoded SML fluent API for the FSM of \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{figure:fsm:a4}}}}\@@caption{{\lx@tag[: ]{Listing\lx@nobreakspace 17}{A Church-encoded SML fluent API for the FSM of \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{figure:fsm:a4}}}}}}}\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{The Church Boolean Encoding Algorithm}
\par As in bit shuffling, the general Church Boolean encoding algorithm starts by converting the input FSM $M$ into an equivalent FSM $M^{\prime}$ with a total transition function and whose number of states is a power of two.
The other generalization steps (to support multiple letters and multiple accepting states) are the same as in bit shuffling (see \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:shuffling:algorithm}).
The full algorithm is shown in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{algorithm:church}.
\par\begin{algorithm}\@@toccaption{{\lx@tag[ ]{{2}}{The Church Boolean algorithm for encoding an FSM as an SML fluent API}}}\@@caption{{\lx@tag[: ]{{Algorithm 2}} {The Church Boolean algorithm for encoding an FSM as an SML fluent API}}}
\centering\fcolorbox{black!25}{white}{
\begin{minipage}{.91\textwidth}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\INPUT FSM $M=\langle Q, \Sigma, q_0, F, \delta\rangle$
\PRINT{The Church Booleans, Church Boolean functions, and Mairson's fanout gate, as written in lines 1--7 of \cref{listing:church:a4}}
\STATE{Construct FSM $M'=\langle Q', \Sigma, q_0, F, \delta'\rangle$ from $M$ by making the transition function $\delta$ total and adding states until $|Q'|$ is a power of two (both using sink states)}
\LET{$n$}{$\log(|Q'|)$}
\LET{$v$}{$\langle b_n, \ldots, b_2, b_1 \rangle$}
\LET{$\beta$}{a mapping $Q'\rightarrow\{0, 1\}^n$ that assigns a unique vector for each state}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{C}{\textasciicircum}{1}]{fun CC f' = f'\ $\beta(q_0)$}}
\FORALL{$\sigma\in\Sigma$}
\LET{$\boldsymbol{f}^\sigma$}{a mapping $\{0, 1\}^n\rightarrow\{0, 1\}^n$ such that $\boldsymbol{f}^\sigma(\beta(q))=\beta(p) \Leftrightarrow\delta_\sigma(q)=p$}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml]{fun\ $\sigma$\ $v$ f' = f'\ $\boldsymbol{f}^\sigma(v)$}}
\ENDFOR\LET{$g^F$}{the (one and only) Boolean function such that $g^F(\beta(q))=1 \Leftrightarrow q \in F$}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{D}{\textdollar}{1}]{fun D\ $v$ = ($(g^F(v))$ 0 "") + 0}}
\end{algorithmic}
\end{minipage}}\@add@centering\end{algorithm}\par As before, the code excerpts in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{algorithm:church} contain mathematical symbols.
These are rendered in text as follows:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@A Boolean bit $b_{i}$ is rendered as \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{T}}}}}}} if $b_{i}=1$, as \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{F}}}}}}} if $b_{i}=0$, or as \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{bi}}}}}}} if it is a variable (lines 9 and 11).
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@A Boolean vector $v=b_{n}\cdots b_{2}b_{1}$ is rendered as a tuple \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}({\@listingGroup{}{$b_{n}$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}\@listingLiterate{\hbox to8.00002pt{.\hss.\hss.}},\textbackslash{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$b_{2}$}},\textbackslash{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$b_{1}$}})}}}}.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@A Boolean vector function $g(v)$ is rendered as a circuit of Church Booleans using Mairson's fanout gate, as described above.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@A Boolean vector function $\boldsymbol{g}(v)$ decomposed to $\langle g_{n}(v),\ldots,g_{2}(v),g_{1}(v)\rangle$ is rendered as a tuple \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}({\@listingGroup{}{$g_{n}(v)$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}\@listingLiterate{\hbox to8.00002pt{.\hss.\hss.}},\textbackslash{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$g_{2}(v)$}},\textbackslash{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$g_{1}(v)$}})}}}}.
\end{itemize}\par\par\@@numbered@section{section}{toc}{Tabulation}
The last encoding technique, \emph{tabulation}, organizes the FSM transition function in a table (2D array) and accesses it at compile time.
Tabulation is a flexible method that can be extended to enforce non-regular API protocols, as discussed in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:beyond}.
We show how to use tabulation to encode a fluent API for $(a^{4})^{*}$, and then, in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{algorithm:tabulation}, we generalize the construction for any FSM.

refnum}{section:shuffling:improvements},wesimplyneedtoremovefromtheFSMeverystatethatcannotreachanacceptingstate.\par Thegeneraltabulationencodingalgorithmisdescribedin\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{algorithm:tabulation}.\par\begin{algorithm}\@@toccaption{{\lx@tag[ ]{{3}}{The tabulation algorithm for encoding an FSM as an SML fluent API}}}\@@caption{{\lx@tag[: ]{{Algorithm 3}} {The tabulation algorithm for encoding an FSM as an SML fluent API}}}
\centering\fcolorbox{black!25}{white}{
\begin{minipage}{.91\textwidth}
\begin{algorithmic}[1]
\INPUT FSM $M=\langle Q, \Sigma, q_0, F, \delta\rangle$
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{D}{\textdollar}{1}]{datatype DD = DD}}
\STATE{Enumerate $Q=\{q_1, q_2, \ldots, q_n\}$}
\FORALL{$j=1, 2, \ldots, n$}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{E}{\hbox to 1em{.\hss.\hss.}}{1}]{fun I$j$ (x1, x2, E, x$n$) = x$j$}}
\ENDFOR\STATE{Enumerate $\Sigma=\{\sigma_1, \sigma_2, \ldots, \sigma_m\}$}
\FORALL{$i=1, 2, \ldots, n$}
\LET{$a$}{\inline[literate=*{D}{\textdollar}{1}]{DD} if $q_i\in F$ or else \inline{()}}
\FORALL{$j=1, 2, \ldots, m$}
\LET{$I^{(j)}$}{$\text{\inline{I$k$}}$ where $\delta(q_i, \sigma_j) = q_k$}
\LET{$I^{(j)}$}{$\text{\inline{()}}$ where $\delta(q_i, \sigma_j)$ is not defined}
\ENDFOR\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{E}{\hbox to 1em{.\hss.\hss.}}{1}]{val e$i$ = ($a$,\ ($I^{(1)}$,\ $I^{(2)}$, E,\ $I^{(m)}$))}}
\ENDFOR\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{E}{\hbox to 1em{.\hss.\hss.}}{1}]{val e = (e1, e2, E, e$n$)}}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{C}{\textasciicircum}{1}]{fun CC f' = f' e$i$} where $q_i$ is the initial state}
\FORALL{$j=1, 2, \ldots, m$}
\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{E}{\hbox to 1em{.\hss.\hss.}}{1}]{val\ $\sigma_j$ (_, (_, E, I, E, _)) f' = f' (I e)} where the inner tuple has $m$ entries and argument \inline{I} appears in the $j$\hh entry}
\ENDFOR\PRINT{\inline[style=sml,literate=*{D}{\textdollar}{1}{E}{\hbox to 1em{.\hss.\hss.}}{1}]{fun D (DD, _) = ()}}
\end{algorithmic}
\end{minipage}}\@add@centering\end{algorithm}\par\par\@@numbered@section{section}{toc}{Beyond Regular Languages}
In \lx@cref{creftypeplural\lx@tilde refnum}{section:shuffling}, \lx@cref{refnum}{section:church} and\lx@nobreakspace\lx@cref{refnum}{section:tabulation} we
described three methods for encoding FSMs as fluent APIs.
These methods can be used to embed regular DSLs in functional
programming languages.
This leaves us wondering about DSLs that are not regular.
It is actually not hard to come up with a fluent API that
recognizes a non-regular language.
For example, the fluent API shown in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:functional:dyck}
recognizes the Dyck language of balanced {L}/{R} brackets,
known to be non-regular.
(This is a functional re-implementation of the object-oriented fluent API
in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:fluent:dyck}.)

}}={\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}\textasciicircum\textasciicircum{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{L}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_comment}{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.5,0.37}{(*does not compile, `(()()(' are not balanced*)}}}\@lst@endline}}{}\@@toccaption{{\lx@tag[ ]{{23}}{An SML fluent API encoding the Dyck language of balanced {L}/{R} brackets}}}\@@caption{{\lx@tag[: ]{Listing\lx@nobreakspace 23}{An SML fluent API encoding the Dyck language of balanced {L}/{R} brackets}}}}}}\par The current section introduces two fluent API encoding techniques
for non-regular languages.
The first technique integrates several simple APIs (as seen in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:functional:dyck})
into one, more complex API.
The second technique generalizes our tabulation encoding
(presented in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:tabulation}) to support all DCFLs.
\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Product APIs}
\par Consider the fluent APIs \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{A2}}}}}}} and \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{A3}}}}}}} shown in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:original:apis}.
These APIs encode the languages $(a^{2})^{*}$ and $(a^{3})^{*}$, respectively, using shuffle encoding (cf.~\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:shuffle:a4}).
\par\par{\@listings{\@@listings@block{24}{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{1\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line Fluent APIs for DCFLs}
\par\cite[citet]{\@@bibref{Authors Phrase1YearPhrase2}{Gil:2019}{\@@citephrase{(}}{\@@citephrase{)}}} presented an algorithm for encoding DCFLs as fluent APIs
in object-oriented languages.
Their algorithm relies on a minimal set of common object-oriented type system features, including
parametric polymorphism and overloading.
For example, a method created by Gil and Roth's construction might look like this:
\par{\@listings{\@@listings@block{26}{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\footnotesize\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{1\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line 1}}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.33}{interface}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{A}}}\textless{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{x}}}\textgreater{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}\textbraceleft\@lst@endline\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{2}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{2\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{2}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line ]{Listing\lx@nobreakspace 26}{The product of the APIs in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:original:apis}}}}}}}We denote the signature of method \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{f}}}}}}} as follows: $$f:A(x)\rightarrow B(C(x),x)$$Note that $f$ explicitly accepts an object of the containing class \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{A}}}\textless{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{x}}}\textgreater}}}} as input.
There are no additional input parameters in the basic encoding.
\par In general, object-oriented fluent API methods have the following form:
\begin{equation}\sigma:\gamma(\boldsymbol{x})\rightarrow\tau\end{equation}where $\sigma$ is the method name, $\gamma$ is the name of the containing class, $\boldsymbol{x}$ are its type parameters, and $\tau$ is the return type.
Type $\tau$ is a (possibly) non-grounded type comprising variables $\boldsymbol{x}$ and other API types.
The fluent API also includes an initial variable:
\begin{equation}\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\textbackslash\textasciicircum\textbackslash\textasciicircum}}}}:t_{0}\end{equation}(for ground type $t_{0}$) and termination methods that return {{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.33}\text{{{{void}}}}}} (unit):
\begin{equation}\text{{\textdollar}}:\gamma(\boldsymbol{x})\rightarrow\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize()}}}}\end{equation}Observe that API method signatures \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:function} are essentially tree rewrite rules.
Thus, we can see a fluent chain of method calls as a series of rewrites of the initial
tree (type) $t_{0}$ of the initial variable \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:initial}.
The termination methods \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:termination} simply separate the API types into
accepting and rejecting types, i.e., by checking whether or not \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar}}$}}}}}} accepts type $\gamma$ as input.
\par For example, the fluent API shown in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:fluent:dyck} in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:preliminaries}, which encodes an {L}/{R} Dyck language, defines the following signatures:
\begin{equation}\begin{array}[]{ll}\text{\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\textbackslash\textasciicircum\textbackslash\textasciicircum}}}}:z&\text{({a})}\\
L:z\rightarrow s(z)&\text{({b})}\\
L:s(x)\rightarrow s(s(x))&\text{({c})}\\
R:s(x)\rightarrow x&\text{({d})}\\
\text{{\$}}:z\rightarrow\text{{()}}&\text{({e})}\end{array}\end{equation}\par The fluent API in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:functional:dyck} overloads method $L$ and, therefore, cannot be directly encoded in a functional programming language.
It is definitely possible, however, to write an equivalent functional API---we saw one in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{listing:functional:dyck}.
We now describe the construction of another functional fluent API for the {L}/{R} Dyck language.
This time, we take a more methodological approach by converting the object-oriented fluent API in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:functional:dyck} into a functional fluent API, and then generalize this method for all DCFLs.
\par The idea is to apply tabulation encoding, presented in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{section:tabulation},
on top of Gil and Roth's construction.
Recall that in the FSM case, we encoded each machine state as a table row (tuple) $$q\Rightarrow\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}({\@listingGroup{}{$a$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}({\@listingGroup{}{$I^{(1)}$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$\ldots$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$I^{(m)}$}}))}}}}}$$where $a\in\{\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}()}}}},\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar\textdollar}}$}}}}}}}\}$ indicates whether state $q$ is accepting and the $I$'s are indexing functions encoding the outgoing $q$ edges.
In the non-regular case, we encode generic types instead of states.
Instead of outgoing edges, we index the rewrite rules of API methods:
{(\hyperref@@ii[eq:functional:dyck]{\ref*{eq:functional:dyck}.b})} is $\rho_{1}$, {(\hyperref@@ii[eq:functional:dyck]{\ref*{eq:functional:dyck}.c})} is $\rho_{2}$,
and {(\hyperref@@ii[eq:functional:dyck]{\ref*{eq:functional:dyck}.d})} is $\rho_{3}$.
In addition, the encoding of generic type $\gamma(\boldsymbol{t})$ recursively includes the encodings of its type arguments $\boldsymbol{t}$.
Thus, type $\gamma(t_{1},\ldots,t_{k})$ is encoded as follows: $$\gamma(t_{1},\ldots,t_{k})\Rightarrow\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}({\@listingGroup{}{$a$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}({\@listingGroup{}{$I^{(1)}$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$\ldots$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$I^{(m)}$}}),{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}({\@listingGroup{}{$e_{1}$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$\ldots$}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{}{$e_{k}$}}))}}}}}$$where {\emph{(i)}} $a$ indicates whether $\gamma$ is accepting, {\emph{(ii)}} $I_{j}$ is the index of $\sigma_{j}:\gamma(\boldsymbol{x})\rightarrow\tau$ in the table,
and {\emph{(iii)}} $e_{1}$ through $e_{k}$ are the encodings of types $t_{1}$ through $t_{k}$.
\par In our example, types $z$ and $s(x)$ are encoded as follows:
\par{\@listings{\@@listings@block{27}{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{1\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line 1}}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_keyword}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.33}{val}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{z}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}={\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}(\textbackslash{\@listingGroup{}{$\ $}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I1}}},{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}()),{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}())\@lst@endline\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{2}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{2\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{2}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line }}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{x}}}))\@lst@endline}}{}\@@toccaption{{\lx@tag[ ]{{27}}{The product of the APIs in \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde $a=\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar\textdollar}}$}}}}}}}$, by {(\hyperref@@ii[eq:functional:dyck]{\ref*{eq:functional:dyck}.e})}, but $s$ is rejecting since function \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar}}$}}}}}}
does not accept $s$.
Functions \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I1}}}}}}}, \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I2}}}}}}}, and \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I3}}}}}}} are the indices of rules $\rho_{1}$, $\rho_{2}$, and
$\rho_{3}$ of the API methods $L$ and $R$.
Since $z:R\rightarrow\tau$ is not defined, we put \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}()}}}} in $R$'s locations in the encoding of $z$.
Finally, type $z$ is non-generic and thus its encoding is a value.
On the other hand, $s$ has a type parameter $x$, so we encode it as a function with a single
parameter that captures $x$'s encoding.
Observe that by encoding generic types as functions, we can create the encodings of complex
types by writing them directly as expressions, e.g., the encoding of type $s(s(z))$
is given by the expression \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{s}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{s}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{z}}}))}}}}.

lor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I1}}}}}}}$, the rewrite function is $\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{I}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{r}}}}}}}=\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{r2}}}}}}}$,
and the resulting expression $\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{r2}}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_space}{ }}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{s}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{z}}})}}}}=\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{s}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{s}}}({\@listingGroup{ltx_lst_identifier}{{z}}}))}}}}$ correctly encodes the application
of $L$ to type $s(z)$ in the original API {(\hyperref@@ii[eq:functional:dyck]{\ref*{eq:functional:dyck}.c})}.
The termination function \hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar}}$}}}}}} simply verifies that $a=\text{\hbox{\pagecolor{lighter-grey}\lstinline{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor{\@listingGroup{}{$\mathstrut$}}{\@listingGroup{}{$\text{{\textdollar\textdollar}}$}}}}}}}$:
\par{\@listings{\@@listings@block{31}{{\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\lst@@@set@language\lst@@@set@numbers\lst@@@set@rulecolor\lst@@@set@frame\footnotesize\@lst@startline{\lx@tags{\lx@tag@intags[]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[autoref]{1\hbox{}}\lx@tag@intags[creftype]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypecap]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypeplural]{}\lx@tag@intags[creftypepluralcap]{}\lx@tag@intags[refnum]{{1}}\lx@tag@intags[typerefnum]{{line
